<span class="ct-node-content">
<span class="ct-node-label">Hauntings</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">What Makes a Haunting Feel Real?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">A haunting is best understood as a repeated report of ghostly or unexplained activity linked to a particular place: apparitions, footsteps, knocks, voices, objects moving, sudden cold spots, smells, or a strong sense that someone unseen is present. The important point is that “haunting” describes a pattern of experience, not a proven cause.</span>
</span>
</a>
